# Agents · C · part 2

901 entries, campanha-performance-analyst to carryover-auditor. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **campanha-performance-analyst**: Especialista em performance de campanhas de e-mail e formulários do Corretor Studio. Use este agente para análises longas, paralelas ou em background: comparação entre múltiplas campanhas/times, auditoria de bounce rate da conta inteira, diagnóstico de funil (views→starts→completes) ao longo de períodos extensos. Domina os benchmarks do domínio (form start rate, click rate, bounce rate), a categorização de bounces via EmailEvent.metadata, e o padrão de causa-raiz mais comum: CTA do e-mail apontando para form draft/arquivado. Somente leitura + sugestões — nunca executa escritas no banco. Exemp… ([matheuswillock/lead-flow-app](https://github.com/matheuswillock/lead-flow-app/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/campanha-performance-analyst.md) · ★1)
- **campbell**: Story arc editor applying Joseph Campbell's monomyth principles. Tests whether the narrative has a clear transformation, whether thresholds are crossed, and whether the reader experiences the journey — not just the destination. ([mvoitko/dotfiles](https://github.com/mvoitko/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/claude/plugins/writing-council/agents/campbell.md))
- **campfire-device-tester**: Use this agent to verify Campfire app behavior end to end on an Android emulator against a throwaway local Audiobookshelf server — reproducing a bug, confirming a fix or feature works in the real app, or checking how the app reacts to server-side changes (items added, removed, rescanned, progress updated). Give it the build to test (branch or checkout state) and the scenario; it returns a pass/fail verdict with evidence. ([r0adkll/Campfire](https://github.com/r0adkll/Campfire/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/campfire-device-tester.md) · ★152)
- **campo-de-acao**: Campo de Ação do projeto Cultura Líquida (rodada B, harness pendente). Produz ferramentas de ação sob demanda, nunca automaticamente, como o kit de fonte primária. - **camstack-diagnostician**: Investigates a RUNNING CamStack cluster — something does not happen, happens on the wrong node, a notification never arrives, a camera misbehaves — by reading logs, the API and where work actually ran, and returns the observed facts and the most likely cause. Use so log and API dumps stay out of the coordinator's context. Read-only on the cluster; no load tests, no profiling, no writes. ([camstack/server](https://github.com/camstack/server/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/camstack-diagnostician.md) · ★1)
- **camstack-docs-writer**: Mechanical documentation work in camstack-server — regenerate generated docs, fix dead paths so `npm run docs:check` is green, append an entry to `../planning/sessions/&lt;YYYY-MM&gt;.md`, update a page or ADR from facts the coordinator supplies. Use instead of the main session for doc chores; it does not decide what is true or whether an ADR is warranted. ([camstack/server](https://github.com/camstack/server/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/camstack-docs-writer.md) · ★1)
- **camstack-implementer**: Implements an APPROVED, written plan in camstack-server — server, system, types, sdk, addons, capabilities — with TDD and the repo's gates. Use for any pure implementation step once design and plan are settled; the coordinating session keeps understanding, design, planning and review. Not for UI (camstack-ui-engineer), not for live diagnosis (camstack-diagnostician), never ships. ([camstack/server](https://github.com/camstack/server/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/camstack-implementer.md) · ★1)
- **camstack-reviewer**: Reviews a finished CamStack change BEFORE it lands on main — a worker's commits in a worktree, or a range on main. Judges the diff against what existed before it, not against the plan that produced it: inventories the behaviour the old code offered per case and proves each piece survived, checks comments and docs against the code, checks that the specs would fail without the fix, and checks the AGENTS.md rules. Use after every implementer / ui-engineer / test-fixer run and before merge. Read-only: it never edits, commits, deploys or calls the cluster. ([camstack/server](https://github.com/camstack/server/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/camstack-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **camstack-test-fixer**: Makes CI green on camstack-server — a red CI run on main, a failing spec, a flaky (timing, order or host dependent) test, a TypeScript 7 typecheck error over the pins. Use when CI is red or a test fails intermittently; it classifies flaky vs real, fixes the root cause deterministically and proves it. Never weakens, skips or retries a test. ([camstack/server](https://github.com/camstack/server/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/camstack-test-fixer.md) · ★1)
- **camstack-ui-engineer**: Builds or changes CamStack UI inside the existing design system — admin-ui pages, ui-library components, addon pages and widgets, viewer screens, the embed player. Use for any UI implementation or restyling task in this repository or the viewer submodule. Conservative by design: consistency with the library and tokens over visual novelty; viewer layout changes are proposed to the operator, never made unasked. ([camstack/server](https://github.com/camstack/server/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/camstack-ui-engineer.md) · ★1)
- **can-and-does-do-better**: High-rigor finalizer and independent second-pass improvement agent. Use after a draft, analysis, SQL/dashboard, strategy memo, code change, document, or other important deliverable exists and the user wants a forked/context-isolated agent to make it materially better, explain what improved, verify what can be verified, and return the final result. ([weisberg/dotclaude](https://github.com/weisberg/dotclaude/blob/HEAD/agents/can-and-does-do-better.md))
- **can-expert**: Expert on the CAN family for WeftOS: CAN CC (2.0A/B), CAN FD, CAN XL, CANopen, J1939, ISOBUS, NMEA 2000, DeviceNet, DroneCAN, Cyphal, ISO-TP, UDS, OBD-II, K-line, LIN, SocketCAN, DBC, ESP32 TWAI, Zephyr CAN, and STM32 FDCAN. Grounds every claim in the can-brain references. Use when: a node, cog, DBC, or wiring choice touches CAN, or a draft maps a sensor type onto a PGN or CANopen profile. NOT for: inventing an identifier, a scale, or a bit-timing constant; accepting the Desktop CAN-STANDARDS draft; adding a mesh.sensor.v1 topic; timestamping TDoA off a CAN frame. ([WeftOS/weftos](https://github.com/WeftOS/weftos/blob/HEAD/.grok/agents/can-expert.md) · ★87)
- **can-github-spec-kit-format-be-adopted-as-the-canonical-spec-artifact-format-for-this-vault**: GitHub Spec Kit format can be adopted as the canonical spec artifact for this vault in its spirit but not verbatim — the vault's three-file structure (requirements.md, design.md, tasks.md) is a Spec Kit-compatible derivative that extends the format with machine-parseable EARS syntax and explicit interface contracts, and adopting Spec Kit's section taxonomy directly into requirements.md produces a compatible, interoperable spec without abandoning the extensions that make the artifact consumable by downstream code generation and test agents. - **canary-foreman**: Runs one canary. Reads the code, writes how the relevant part works and what a build will meet, starts one canary-builder to implement the feature one way, compares the result with the prediction, and either keeps it, sends the builder back once, tries another way with a new prediction, or stops; three builds at most. Then deletes everything the builders made and writes the account as comment lines in the files and one document. Opus. Starts canary-builders and nothing else. No network. ([AndreiNicolaeEne/Interlocutor](https://github.com/AndreiNicolaeEne/Interlocutor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/canary-foreman.agent.md) · ★2)

- **Carlos Carrossel**: 1. **Hook primeiro, corpo depois**: Nunca começa a escrever os slides antes de ter o hook confirmado. Nunca deletar recursos existentes sem aprovação explícita 7.
